# MailBot - Epic Breakdown

## Overview

This document provides the complete epic and story breakdown for MailBot, decomposing the requirements from the PRD, Architecture, and the originating brainstorming session into implementable stories.

## Requirements Inventory

### Functional Requirements

#### F1 — Outlook Sync (FR-1.x)

- **FR-1.1** — Cron-pull sync every 4 minutes (aligned to Anthropic cache TTL — Rule M); fetches Graph delta since last sync; writes new/changed/deleted email rows to local SQLite.
- **FR-1.2** — Delta-only fetch via Graph delta link stored in `sync_state`. First-time sync of large inbox may take 10–30 minutes; subsequent syncs are seconds.
- **FR-1.3** — Soft-delete preservation: deleted emails marked `deleted_at` in SQL rather than hard-deleted; raw fields preserved for audit.
- **FR-1.4** — Idempotent re-runs keyed on `(graph_id, change_marker)`. Running sync twice with no inbox changes produces zero writes and zero LLM calls.
- **FR-1.5** — Sync-health surfacing: if sync fails to complete for > 1 hour, urgent Discord notification fires; condition detectable by `mailbot status` CLI inside 10 seconds.
- **FR-1.6** — Headless OAuth via refresh-token copy: first-time auth happens on dev box; refresh token hand-copied into VPS `.env`; re-auth procedure documented in `docs/auth-recovery.md`.
- **FR-1.7** — `has_attachments` only at v1: attachment metadata (boolean + count) stored; bodies not fetched. Full attachment policy deferred.

#### F2 — Ingest Pipeline (FR-2.x)

- **FR-2.1** — Derived-field columns first-class: each derived signal (classification coarse + fine, importance, summary, sensitivity, suggested action, embedding) in dedicated SQL columns, each carrying `*_prompt_v`, `*_conf`, `*_model`, `*_at` companion metadata.
- **FR-2.2** — Idempotency keyed on `sha256(body) + prompt_version + model + task_type` (Rule K). Re-running pipeline at same prompt version is free.
- **FR-2.3** — Fixed pipeline ordering per email: `sensitivity_class → coarse_class → (fine_class if human) → summary_short → importance_scoring → action_extraction → embedding`. Hard invariant: no Router call for any other task on `email_id` until `emails.sensitivity_at IS NOT NULL` — Router enforces with `RouterError(code="sensitivity_not_classified")`.
- **FR-2.4** — Backpressure ceiling: if unprocessed queue > 500 emails, pipeline processes in 100-batch chunks with pauses to keep interactive lane responsive.
- **FR-2.5** — Sensitivity is local-only: sensitivity classifier never escapes Qwen 3B regardless of policy — hard-coded in Router (Rule Q).
- **FR-2.6** — Targeted re-derivation: opt-in batch re-derives selected columns for affected rows when prompt version bumped; off by default; explicit `mailbot` CLI invocation.
- **FR-2.7** — Senders and threads upsert on every new email. On first sender contact, one-line `sender_reputation_summary` generated by Qwen and cached forever (Rule A).

#### F3 — Router & Tiered LLM (FR-3.x)

- **FR-3.1** — Three-tier model stack at v1: Qwen 2.5 3B Instruct (Q4_K_M) via Ollama (free) + Claude Haiku 4.5 `claude-haiku-4-5-20251001` (cheap) + Claude Opus 4.7 `claude-opus-4-7` (premium). Adding a fourth model is a single `policy.yaml` config change.
- **FR-3.2** — Single agent verb interface: `ask_router(task_type, content, force_model=None, max_cost_usd=None) → RouterResult`. No direct Ollama or Anthropic calls anywhere, including the eval runner.
- **FR-3.3** — Static `policy.yaml` table with override: per-task `{model, prompt_version, escalate, max_tokens_out, lane, sensitivity}`. Per-call overrides allowed but always logged with `model_chosen_reason="override"`.
- **FR-3.4** — Layered failure handling: call → timeout (30s local / 60s API) → schema validation → single retry with stricter prompt → escalate-to-next-tier (if `escalate=true`) → return `RouterResult(ok=False, error=RouterError(...))`. Stable error codes: `schema_validation_failed`, `timeout`, `budget_exceeded`, `per_call_threshold_exceeded`, `provider_error`, `monthly_budget_exceeded`, `degraded_mode_blocked`, `loop_detected`, `sensitivity_blocks_api`.
- **FR-3.5** — Lane separation: `interactive` and `batch` lanes; interactive preempts batch. Per-lane rate limits (chat 60/hr, ingest 300/hr, escalations 20/hr).
- **FR-3.6** — Anthropic prompt caching is primary: all API calls mark SYSTEM block as `cache_control: ephemeral`. Cache warmer pings high-volume tasks every 4 minutes (aligned to 5-min TTL). Per-task `cache_hit_rate` tracked; regressions alert.
- **FR-3.7** — Response cache: identical `hash(prompt+model+temp)` calls return cached results. SQL-backed, TTL configurable per task in `policy.yaml`.
- **FR-3.8** — Anti-loop kill-switch: prompt-hash repeated > 10× in 5 min refuses with `loop_detected`. `/pause` chat command halts Router until `/resume`.
- **FR-3.9** — Audit log: every call writes one `router_calls` row (`task_type`, `prompt_version`, `model_chosen`, `model_chosen_reason`, `tokens_in/out/cached_in`, `cost_usd_estimated`, `latency_ms`, `outcome`, `caller_verb`, `email_id`). Ground truth for cost analysis, drift detection, routing tuning.
- **FR-3.10** — Hermes auxiliary tasks (context compression, title generation) route through the Router via `/v1/chat/completions` endpoint; Rule Ω extends to Hermes's own LLM work.

#### F4 — Conversational Control (FR-4.x)

- **FR-4.1** — Discord adapter as gateway: Hermes Discord adapter bound to private bot in server Adam shares with MailBot. No web UI, no email replies to MailBot itself.
- **FR-4.2** — Conversational queries: Adam asks ("show me unread from today", "what's important this week", "anything from the lawyer", "summarize that thread") → MailBot responds via Router-routed verb calls within latency bound stated in NFR-PERF-1.
- **FR-4.3** — Reference resolution: pronouns and ellipsis ("that one", "the lawyer", "Marc's last email") resolve against recent context + memory. Acceptance bound: ≥ 90% correct resolution on dedicated reference-resolution slice (~20 items) in `email_corpus_v1.jsonl` at M4.1.
- **FR-4.4** — Draft reply flow: "Draft a reply to that" → Opus drafts → MailBot shows draft → Adam edits or approves → cooling-off → send. Draft generation, tone mirroring, multi-turn refinement are tier-1 product capabilities.
- **FR-4.5** — Slash commands: `/cost`, `/pause`, `/resume`, `/cancel <action_id>`, `/mute <category>`, `/label <recent>`. Explicit, terse, bypass natural-language ambiguity.
- **FR-4.6** — Persona consistency: MailBot speaks as a defender (conservative, quiet, asks before destructive actions). Persona encoded in `SOUL.md`; consistent across all chat surfaces (DM, digest, alerts, slash-command replies).
- **FR-4.7** — Chat input redactor: Adam's chat messages scrubbed for token-shaped strings (JWTs, `sk-…`, hex blobs) before entering agent memory or being forwarded to API. Same redactor runs on memory exports.
- **FR-4.8** — `[ASSUMPTION]` Slash command parity in DM and channel: commands work identically in DM and shared server channel.

#### F5 — Authorized Actions (FR-5.x)

- **FR-5.1** — Four-tier authorization model: Tier 0 (free — read SQL, LLM calls, draft generation, chat notifications, derived-field writes); Tier 1 (auto-revertible, logged — mark read/unread, local categories, move to `MailBot-Triage`); Tier 2 (batch approval — archive, mark junk, move to user folders, unsubscribe); Tier 3 (per-action confirmation — delete, send, modify rules/filters, touch delegated accounts).
- **FR-5.2** — Tier-jumping by scoped grant: Adam grants authorization in chat ("you may delete the 47 spam emails from Acme; valid for 1 hour"). Grant cached against `(action_type, email_ids, expiry)`; invalid after expiry.
- **FR-5.3** — Cooling-off on sends: approved outbound emails sit in `pending_send` for 60 seconds (configurable) before leaving. `/cancel <action_id>` during window kills send.
- **FR-5.4** — Hard daily send cap: MailBot sends at most 20 outbound emails per day regardless of authorization. Hijack-resistance.
- **FR-5.5** — Queued-intent write-back: all actions enter `pending_actions`, drained by separate Python worker which performs second authorization check at drain time. Benefits: retry, audit, dry-run, undo.
- **FR-5.6** — Tier enforcement is verb-API, not agent: agent cannot promote action's tier — verb refuses tier upgrades.
- **FR-5.7** — Sensitive-content API override handshake: when agent needs to escalate `sensitive`-classified email to Anthropic (medical reply, legal summary), user explicitly confirms in chat → mints per-session confirmation token scoped to `(email_id, task_type)`. Token consumed by single Router call; subsequent calls require fresh confirmation. Router refuses sensitive-to-API calls without valid token. `confidential` emails admit no override.

#### F6 — Cost Governance (FR-6.x)

- **FR-6.1** — Per-call output cap: every Router call enforces `max_tokens_out` from `policy.yaml`. Default 4000; task-overridable.
- **FR-6.2** — Daily soft warning: when today's cumulative spend exceeds $2, MailBot sends one-time chat warning (does not block calls). Threshold in `policy.yaml`.
- **FR-6.3** — Monthly hard cap: when month's cumulative spend exceeds $30, system enters **degraded mode** (Opus → Haiku, Haiku → Qwen demotion chain; `force_model="claude-opus-4-7"` requires explicit chat confirmation; one urgent notification fires once). Persists until next month or manual `/budget reset` slash command.
- **FR-6.4** — Per-call refusal threshold: any call estimated at > $0.20 refused with `per_call_threshold_exceeded` unless `force=true`. Catches "agent stuffed entire inbox into one prompt" bugs.
- **FR-6.5** — Hourly anomaly detection: hourly call volume baselined; significant deviations alert Adam in chat before budget breach.
- **FR-6.6** — Default-cheapest with annotated escalation: every task in `policy.yaml` defaults to Qwen 3B; each escalation to Haiku or Opus carries inline `notes` justification AND a DEMOTION HYPOTHESIS tested by F8 benchmarks. Every Opus assignment cites a benchmark run.
- **FR-6.7** — `/cost` transparency: Adam can query spend anytime — today's $, this month's $, cap, per-task breakdown, per-model breakdown, cache hit rate. Sourced from `router_calls`.

#### F7 — Observability & Trust (FR-7.x)

- **FR-7.1** — `mailbot status` CLI: SSH into VPS; returns within 10 seconds — container health, last successful sync, unprocessed email count, pending actions awaiting auth, today's spend vs cap, cache hit rate this week, last 5 errored `router_calls`.
- **FR-7.2** — `mailbot logs` and `mailbot pause/resume`: tail Docker logs; emergency stop from VPS shell. `/pause` from chat does same.
- **FR-7.3** — Structured JSON logging: all services log structured JSON to stdout with timestamp, level, module, message, context. Errors carry sanitized stacks (no API keys, no Graph URLs with tokens — Rule F).
- **FR-7.4** — Notification tiers: **urgent** (push immediately — Tier 3 auth, anomaly, kill-switch, budget breach, failed sync > 1h); **important** (batched into 08:00 digest — Tier 2 approvals, drift report, sampling requests); **informational** (pull-only via slash commands); **silent** (log-only — routine classifications, cache pings, sync events).
- **FR-7.5** — Anti-fatigue: quiet hours 22:00–08:00 (no non-urgent); same-kind notifications 5+/hour collapse to one; `/mute <category>` silences until `/unmute`; if response rate < 30% for a week, ONE self-reflection message ("I'm becoming noisy") then **urgent-only posture** until any slash command is issued.
- **FR-7.6** — Trust-signal opt-out: Adam can permanently mute self-reflection (configurable in `policy.yaml`). Default enabled.
- **FR-7.7** — Daily digest: at 08:00 Adam's timezone, Hermes cron-with-agent calls `compose_digest()` verb (reads pre-computed columns — Rule A; no body re-derivation), then assembles digest as light templating + light synthesis. Routing via `policy.yaml[daily_digest]` (Qwen 3B in v0). Contents: unread grouped by importance, pending Tier 2 batches awaiting approval, weekly drift/sampling artifacts when scheduled.

#### F8 — Eval & Calibration (FR-8.x)

- **FR-8.1** — Frozen, versioned eval corpus: `evals/email_corpus_v1.jsonl` — ~100 hand-labeled real emails covering 8 task families (transactional, newsletter, human-personal, human-professional, cold outreach, spam-like, notification, edge cases — calendar invites, password resets). Personally labeled by Adam (~3–5 hours one-time).
- **FR-8.2** — Benchmark runner: `benchmark/runner.py` executes every `(eval_item × task × model × prompt_version)` combo through Router with `force_model`. Records in `benchmark_runs` table. Resumable; estimates cost upfront; asks confirmation if > $5.
- **FR-8.3** — Scorer (objective + subjective): classification → exact match → accuracy/precision/recall/confusion. Structured extraction → field-level match. Subjective (drafts, summaries) → 20 hand-anchored examples + strong-model auto-eval calibrated against anchors.
- **FR-8.4** — Report generator: per-task table of model × (accuracy, p95 latency, cost per 100 calls); 3B failure modes with examples; Pareto frontier per task; calibration suggestions flagging each `policy.yaml` row as "DEMOTE valid / invalid / PROMOTE needed."
- **FR-8.5** — Continuous eval through production sampling: every Sunday — sample 5 random `router_calls` (low-confidence weighted), DM Adam in Discord with "did I get this right?" emoji-reaction prompts. Answers append to `evals/email_corpus_v2.jsonl`. Corpus grows weekly.
- **FR-8.6** — Drift detection: every Sunday — distribution of week's `class_coarse` outputs compared to eval corpus distribution. KL divergence > threshold → drift alert in chat.
- **FR-8.7** — Shadow-mode rollout for subjective tasks: new prompt versions for draft/summary tasks generate alongside production version (not used) for configurable window; compared later. Catches silent quality regressions (Rule O).

### NonFunctional Requirements

#### Privacy & Sensitivity (NFR-PRIV-0..4)

- **NFR-PRIV-0** — Privacy posture: VPS-local is the trust boundary. Qwen 3B on VPS may see every email category including sensitive and confidential; what crosses to Anthropic is what Rule Q gates.
- **NFR-PRIV-1** — Three-tier sensitivity model: every email classified at ingest as `normal` / `sensitive` / `confidential`. When in doubt, default to `sensitive` (cautious bias).
- **NFR-PRIV-2** — Routing by sensitivity: `normal` (Qwen/Haiku/Opus allowed; full body indexed and logged); `sensitive` (Qwen-only by default; API only with `force_model` + per-session chat confirmation; memory stores summary + IDs; body purged after derivation; logs hash content); `confidential` (Qwen-only, no exception; memory stores metadata only; body purged after derivation; logs metadata only).
- **NFR-PRIV-3** — User-configurable sensitivity patterns: regex / sender-domain / keyword rules in `sensitivity_patterns.yaml` *force* `sensitive` or `confidential` regardless of classifier output.
- **NFR-PRIV-4** — Chat-input redactor: token-shaped strings scrubbed from inputs; same redactor applies to memory exports and trajectory dumps.

#### Secrets Management (NFR-SEC-1..6 — Rule F)

- **NFR-SEC-1** — Secrets in `.env` only, `chmod 600`, owned by service user. Never in source, prompts, skills, configs, memory.
- **NFR-SEC-2** — Anthropic API key isolated to Router process. Hermes never has key in its process memory. Only `ask_router` verb's process reads `os.environ["ANTHROPIC_API_KEY"]`.
- **NFR-SEC-3** — Agent has no filesystem path to secrets: tool-layer denylist on `.env`, `*.key`, `*.pem`, SSH dirs, SQL DSN — enforced outside agent's control.
- **NFR-SEC-4** — Sanitized error returns: verbs sanitize all error messages and return values before they reach the agent. No raw stack traces, no URLs with tokens.
- **NFR-SEC-5** — `get_secret(name)` indirection: single function for all secret reads; swapping `.env` → vault later is localized change.
- **NFR-SEC-6** — Backups exclude secrets: automated backups never include `.env`.

#### Reliability & Operations (NFR-OPS-1..6)

- **NFR-OPS-1** — Dev-prod parity via containers: local dev and VPS prod run same Docker images via `docker compose`. Only volume bind paths and `.env` values differ (Rule S).
- **NFR-OPS-2** — Single-command deploy: `make deploy` does build → save → scp → load → rolling restart of `mailbot-api` only → health check → log tail. Hermes and Ollama containers updated separately via `docker compose pull` (Rule T).
- **NFR-OPS-3** — State survives containers: all state on named volumes (`mailbot_db`, `mailbot_ollama`, `mailbot_hermes_data`, `mailbot_logs`). `docker compose down && up` is non-event (Rule V).
- **NFR-OPS-4** — Health endpoints: `mailbot-api` exposes `/health` and `/v1/health`. Deploy aborts on health check failure (Rule W).
- **NFR-OPS-5** — Nightly backups: host cron on VPS does SQLite `.backup`, tarballs config, optionally rsyncs to off-site B2. Retention 14 daily + 8 weekly. Backups exclude `.env`.
- **NFR-OPS-6** — Resilience via Hermes fallback: Hermes `fallback_providers` configured with direct Anthropic Opus as safety net if Router is down. Bypasses cost discipline by design — emergency only (Rule X).

#### Performance (NFR-PERF-1..4)

- **NFR-PERF-1** — Chat response p95 ≤ 5s for typical queries (verb call + Qwen/Haiku response). Opus draft generation exempt (target p95 ≤ 15s).
- **NFR-PERF-2** — Sync cadence: 4 minutes (aligned to Anthropic 5-min cache TTL — Rule M). Inbox freshness floor ~4 min.
- **NFR-PERF-3** — Qwen 3B latency budget on 2-vCPU CPU-only: interactive tasks routed to Qwen must show p95 ≤ 5s on VPS hardware; otherwise PROMOTION HYPOTHESIS to Haiku validated by FR-8.2 benchmarks.
- **NFR-PERF-4** — Ingest throughput: pipeline processes 100 emails per batch, max 100 emails per 5-min ingest cron run, with backpressure if queue > 500.

#### Deployability & Hardware (NFR-HW-1..3)

- **NFR-HW-1** — Target: Hostinger KVM 2 (2 vCPU, 8 GB RAM, 100 GB NVMe, 8 TB bandwidth, no GPU). Hardware upgrade declined; design must fit.
- **NFR-HW-2** — Local LLM RAM budget: 4.5–5.5 GB after OS, SQL, Python services, Hermes. Determines single 3B model commitment.
- **NFR-HW-3** — No 7B fallback at v1: binary local-or-API design. Adding middle model is `policy.yaml` change.

#### Persona & Voice (NFR-PERSONA-1..3)

- **NFR-PERSONA-1** — Defender, not assistant: MailBot's voice is conservative, quiet, asks before destructive actions, shows reasoning when proposing actions. Anchored in `SOUL.md`.
- **NFR-PERSONA-2** — Behavioral anti-patterns: never send without per-message authorization; never delete without per-action authorization; never quote sensitive content outside chat; never produce noisy notifications.
- **NFR-PERSONA-3** — Operational rules in `AGENTS.md`: how Rules J/N/P/R manifest as agent behavior (hydration discipline, tier-aware proposals, notification tiering, cost-aware verb choice).

### Additional Requirements

These are technical requirements from the Architecture document that affect implementation. They are **not** restatements of FRs — they are architectural decisions (D1–D14) and starter-template choices that downstream stories must implement.

#### Starter Template / Bootstrap (Architecture §2)

- **AR-BOOT-1** — **Hand-bootstrap (no template generator):** the 3-container Docker Compose architecture is the starter; no Yeoman/cookiecutter generator runs. Build-order M1.1 is the bootstrap story. Initialization sequence: `git init` → `python -m venv .venv` → pin `requirements.txt` → scaffold package layout → drop in Dockerfile + compose → smoke test (`docker compose up`; `curl localhost:8000/health`).
- **AR-BOOT-2** — **Pinned runtime versions (May 2026):** Python 3.12 baseline; `pip` + `venv`; FastAPI 0.136.1; uvicorn (latest); Pydantic v2 (bundled with FastAPI); MCP Python SDK 1.27.2; `ollama` Python 0.6.2; `anthropic` Python 0.105.2; stdlib `sqlite3`; **numpy** (latest, ~30 MB resident — cosine for embedding search, percentiles + KL divergence for FR-8.6 drift detection, precision/recall for FR-8.3 scorer); **matplotlib** (latest, ~50 MB resident — PNG chart rendering posted to Discord); pytest + pytest-asyncio; ruff (replaces black+isort+flake8); mypy strict. **pandas explicitly deferred** — most tabular needs are satisfied by raw SQL through `db/queries.py`; revisit only if a future story justifies it.
- **AR-BOOT-3** — **Repo skeleton:** package layout per Architecture §2 (mailbot_api/{db,router,prompts,verbs,sync,ingest,actions,sensitivity,observability,notifications}, evals/, benchmark/, scripts/, hermes-config/, docker/, router/policy.yaml, tests/{unit,integration,fixtures}, docs/). Makefile targets: `build`, `deploy`, `logs`, `status`, `local`, `backup`, `test`, `lint`.

#### Privacy Mechanism (D1 + D12)

- **AR-D1-1** — **Inline `confirmation_token` parameter** on `ask_router(task_type, content, force_model=None, confirmation_token=None, max_cost_usd=None) → RouterResult`. Router precondition layer checks token against server-side mint registry keyed by `(token, email_id, task_type)`; marks consumed on call; refuses sensitive-to-API calls without valid token.
- **AR-D1-2** — **`mint_sensitivity_token(email_id, task_type) → token`** Tier-0 verb. Gates on recent in-chat user confirmation event recorded by Hermes (`/confirm <email_id> <task_type>` slash command or natural-language affirmation Hermes maps to the verb). Mint event logged structured-JSON; consume event becomes `router_calls` row write. `confidential` emails admit no override — verb refuses to mint.
- **AR-D12-1** — **In-memory token storage** in `mailbot-api` worker process (Router lives there), keyed by token string, TTL eviction (default 10 min from mint). Tokens die on process restart by design (forces re-confirmation).
- **AR-D12-2** — **Audit columns on `router_calls`:** `sensitivity_grant_id` (nullable, present only when call consumed grant) + `sensitivity_grant_minted_at`. Mint events emit structured log line; consume events become row write.

#### Routing Discipline (D2 + D10 + D11)

- **AR-D2-1** — **Hermes aux-task routing enforcement (config-only).** Hermes configured with `provider.base_url = http://mailbot-api:8000/v1`, `auxiliary.compression.provider = custom`, `auxiliary.title_generation.provider = custom`. All Hermes-internal LLM work flows through Router by configuration.
- **AR-D2-2** — **Drift detection** rather than network policy: `mailbot status` includes "Hermes aux traffic last 24h" line; if zero aux calls recorded from Hermes for ≥ 24h, raise informational alert. Router records `caller_origin` (`hermes-aux-compression`, `hermes-aux-title`, `verb-ask-router`, `benchmark-runner`, `cache-warmer`) on every `router_calls` row.
- **AR-D10-1** — **Two asyncio queues with strict priority:** `interactive_q` (high) and `batch_q` (low). Single worker pool always drains `interactive_q` first; pulls from `batch_q` only when `interactive_q` empty.
- **AR-D10-2** — **Rate limits enforced at enqueue:** chat 60/hr, ingest 300/hr, escalations 20/hr — exceeded → immediate refusal with `RouterError(code="rate_limited")`.
- **AR-D10-3** — **Per-provider concurrency semaphore** (default 4 concurrent Anthropic calls) to avoid surprise 429s independent of lane priority.
- **AR-D11-1** — **`policy.yaml` file-watch hot-reload via `watchfiles`** with validation-or-no-swap: on change → re-read → validate against `PolicyTable` Pydantic schema → atomic swap of in-memory policy only on success. Invalid YAML/schema mismatch leaves running policy in place and logs loudly.
- **AR-D11-2** — **Mid-call race acceptable:** each Router call captures its policy snapshot at dispatch; in-flight calls finish under pre-swap policy. `router_calls` records `prompt_version` and `model_chosen` making swap observable post-hoc.

#### Sync ↔ Actions (D4 + D5 + D9)

- **AR-D4-1** — **Hybrid sync conflict policy:** Tier-3 actions enforce strict ETag (`change_marker`) match at drain time — if state drifted, refuse with `RouterError(code="state_drift_etag")` and notify urgently. Tier-1 and Tier-2 apply lenient rules per action-type roster.
- **AR-D4-2** — **Three lenient rules for Tier-1/2:** (1) target deleted (`emails.deleted_at IS NOT NULL`) → action → `status="failed"` with `failure_reason="target_deleted"`; silent log (Tier-1) or next digest (Tier-2); no urgent notification. (2) Target moved but exists → execute (idempotent actions like `mark_read` still valid). (3) Target moved AND action is folder-move → re-resolve target folder; execute if still semantically valid (target ≠ current), else `failure_reason="state_drift_noop"`. No Graph round-trip — local `emails.deleted_at` authoritative.
- **AR-D5-1** — **Error-classified write-back retry chain:** Graph 429/503/timeout → exponential backoff (1s, 4s, 16s), max 3 retries. Graph 4xx (non-429) → immediate `failed`, no retry. Graph 5xx (non-503) → 1 retry then `failed`. Network errors treated as transient.
- **AR-D5-2** — **Tier-3 send-budget circuit breaker:** failed Tier-3 sends consume daily 20-send budget at terminal-fail, never silently. `pending_actions.budget_consumed` boolean flips on either successful leave or terminal fail.
- **AR-D5-3** — **`status="failed"` rows stay in `pending_actions`** for audit and manual replay. `mailbot status` reports counts; `mailbot replay <action_id>` re-queues. No separate dead-letter table.
- **AR-D5-4** — **Failure notification tiers:** Tier-1 silent log; Tier-2 in next daily digest; Tier-3 urgent immediately.
- **AR-D9-1** — **OAuth refresh-token rotation persists to `oauth_state` SQLite table** with single row keyed by `provider="microsoft_graph"`. Columns: `refresh_token`, `access_token`, `access_expires_at`, `last_rotated_at`, `rotation_count`. Sync layer reads from `oauth_state` if present; falls back to `.env` only on first run.
- **AR-D9-2** — **`.env` becomes bootstrap seed** — used once to populate row, then `oauth_state` is runtime source of truth. Documented in `docs/auth-recovery.md`. Rotation events emit structured log line (info-tier). Rotation failures (`invalid_grant`) escalate to urgent chat alert, picked up by FR-1.5's sync-health alarm if recovery > 1h. Backups include `oauth_state` automatically.

#### Authorization Detail (D6)

- **AR-D6-1** — **`ActionType` enum + `tier_for(action_type)` lookup** in `mailbot_api/actions/types.py` as single source of truth. `propose_action(email_id, action_type, **payload)` verb consults lookup at insert and refuses any agent-claimed tier override (FR-5.6 in code).
- **AR-D6-2** — **Action-type roster (19 types pinned to 4 tiers):**
  - **Tier 0** — `read_sql`, `ask_router`, `generate_draft`, `send_chat_notification`, `write_derived_field`
  - **Tier 1** — `mark_read`, `mark_unread`, `add_local_category`, `remove_local_category`, `move_to_triage_folder`
  - **Tier 2** — `archive`, `mark_junk`, `move_to_user_folder`, `unsubscribe`, `move_to_inbox`
  - **Tier 3** — `delete`, `send_reply`, `send_new_email`, `send_forward`, `reply_to_inactive_thread`, `modify_inbox_rule`, `modify_outlook_filter`, `touch_delegated_mailbox`
- **AR-D6-3** — **Cross-cutting action properties:** `reversibility_window_hours` (24h for Tier-1 via pre-state snapshot in `action_history`; not auto-revertible Tier-2/3); `change_marker_required` (true Tier-3 only per D4); `budget_against` (`daily_send_cap_20` for all `send_*` and `reply_to_inactive_thread`; null otherwise); `requires_sensitivity_token` (true for any Tier-2/3 action generating outbound content from `sensitive` email; `confidential` admits no Tier-2/3 outbound action).
- **AR-D6-4** — **`action_grants` table** (new): `(id, action_type, email_ids JSON, expires_at, minted_at, revoked_at)`. Grants scoped to specific action type — `delete` grant does not authorize `send_reply`.

#### Runtime Mechanics (D3 + D7 + D8 + D13 + D14)

- **AR-D3-1** — **Daily digest is hybrid:** `compose_digest()` verb on `mailbot-api` returns structured payload (unread groups by importance, pending Tier-2 batch counts, weekly drift/sampling artifacts) by reading cached projections only (Rule A enforced — summaries from `summary_short` field, no body re-derivation). Hermes cron-with-agent at 08:00 calls `compose_digest()`, makes 1 Qwen call (≤ 200 tokens) for persona-voiced intro paragraph, wraps intro around structured payload, posts to Discord. Response-cached against input hash.
- **AR-D7-1** — **Two processes inside `mailbot-api` container:** (1) `uvicorn mailbot_api.main:app` — FastAPI HTTP + MCP server (chat-serving). (2) `python -m mailbot_api.worker` — background tasks (sync, ingest pipeline, drainer, cache warmer). Both started by Dockerfile entrypoint (worker backgrounded; uvicorn foreground). One container, one Docker service.
- **AR-D7-2** — **Inter-process status via `worker_health` SQLite table;** FastAPI reads it for `mailbot status` and `/v1/health`. Cross-process signaling (e.g., "policy.yaml changed") via SQLite event row or filesystem signal file.
- **AR-D8-1** — **SQLite WAL mode + `busy_timeout=5000` + all writes through `run_in_executor`.** Reads stay on asyncio event loop (sub-ms in WAL); writes go through executor so slow write/checkpoint doesn't stall chat.
- **AR-D8-2** — **Pragmas applied on every connection:** `journal_mode=WAL`, `synchronous=NORMAL`, `busy_timeout=5000`, `foreign_keys=ON`. One connection per task (no shared connections across awaits); short-lived; closed promptly.
- **AR-D13-1** — **Cron split between Hermes and `mailbot-api` internal scheduler:** Hermes cron (agent-involving) — daily digest 08:00, weekly drift report Sun 09:00, weekly sampling Sun 09:30. `mailbot-api` internal scheduler (LLM-free critical infra) — sync every 4 min, cache warmer every 4 min, ingest pipeline every 5 min, pending_actions drainer (continuous loop).
- **AR-D14-1** — **stdlib `sqlite3` + raw SQL migrations.** No SQLAlchemy, no `aiosqlite`, no ORM. Schema-as-code in `schema.sql` + numbered migration files (`001_init.sql`, `002_oauth_state.sql`, …) in `mailbot_api/db/migrations/`. Migrations applied at startup if not recorded in `_migrations` table. Pydantic models on data boundaries; raw SQL for internal queries centralized in `mailbot_api/db/queries.py`.

#### Schema (new tables introduced by architecture)

- **AR-SCHEMA-1** — **`router_calls` additions:** `caller_origin`, `sensitivity_grant_id` (nullable), `sensitivity_grant_minted_at` (nullable).
- **AR-SCHEMA-2** — **`emails.change_marker` column** (ETag/etag-equivalent, already implicit in FR-1.4's idempotency on `(graph_id, change_marker)`).
- **AR-SCHEMA-3** — **`pending_actions` schema:** `id`, `email_id`, `action_type` (enum), `tier` (1/2/3, derived from action_type at insert), `payload` (JSON), `proposed_at`, `proposed_by_grant_id` (nullable), `change_marker_at_propose`, `status` (`pending`/`cooling_off`/`draining`/`applied`/`failed`), `retry_count`, `failure_reason`, `terminal_at`, `budget_consumed` (bool).
- **AR-SCHEMA-4** — **`action_grants`** (new): `(id, action_type, email_ids JSON, expires_at, minted_at, revoked_at)`.
- **AR-SCHEMA-5** — **`action_history`** (new): `(action_id, pre_state JSON, applied_at, reverted_at nullable)` — supports Tier-1 24h revert.
- **AR-SCHEMA-6** — **`oauth_state`** (new): single row keyed by `provider`; `(refresh_token, access_token, access_expires_at, last_rotated_at, rotation_count)`.
- **AR-SCHEMA-7** — **`worker_health`** (new): `(component, last_heartbeat_at, last_outcome, last_error)` — components: `sync`, `cache_warmer`, `drainer`, `ingest_pipeline`.
- **AR-SCHEMA-8** — **`benchmark_runs`** (new): per-call record for the benchmark runner; ground truth for F8 calibration suggestions.

#### Stable Error Codes (new, beyond FR-3.4 base set)

- **AR-ERR-1** — `rate_limited` — lane rate limit exceeded at enqueue (D10).
- **AR-ERR-2** — `state_drift_etag` — Tier-3 change_marker mismatch at drain (D4).
- **AR-ERR-3** — `target_deleted` — drain found target removed; treated per tier per D4.
- **AR-ERR-4** — `state_drift_noop` — lenient-policy resolution decided to no-op (D4).
- **AR-ERR-5** — `needs_sensitivity_confirmation` — sensitive-to-API call attempted without valid token (D1).
- **AR-ERR-6** — `sensitivity_not_classified` — Router call attempted on email_id before sensitivity column written (FR-2.3 invariant).

#### Implementation Patterns & Enforcement (Architecture §4)

- **AR-PAT-1** — **Five hard code boundaries enforced by lint + review:**
  1. `router/models.py` is the only file importing `ollama` or `anthropic` (Rule I).
  2. `mailbot_api/sync/graph_client.py` is the only file importing `requests` for `graph.microsoft.com` (Rule B).
  3. `mailbot_api/db/queries.py` is the only file with SQL literals (Rule C).
  4. `mailbot_api/config.py` is the only file reading `os.environ` (Rule F).
  5. `mailbot_api/observability/audit.py` is the only file writing to `router_calls`.
- **AR-PAT-2** — **Naming conventions enforced:** snake_case everywhere (DB tables plural snake_case; FK columns `<table_singular>_id`; companion fields `<field>_prompt_v/_conf/_model/_at`; timestamps `*_at`; booleans `is_*`/`has_*`); Python `snake_case` modules/functions, `PascalCase` classes, `UPPER_SNAKE_CASE` constants, `<Noun>In`/`<Noun>Out` Pydantic verb-boundary models; MCP tool names match verb function name exactly.
- **AR-PAT-3** — **Format invariants:** `RouterResult` and `RouterError` Pydantic shapes pinned (Architecture §4 Format Patterns); UTC ISO-8601 with `Z` suffix end-to-end (`datetime.now(timezone.utc)` always — never `datetime.utcnow()`); snake_case on every JSON boundary; structured JSON log line shape pinned (`ts`, `level`, `module`, `event`, `task_type`, `model`, `caller_origin`, `email_id`, `cost_usd`, `latency_ms`).
- **AR-PAT-4** — **Error-as-data discipline:** verbs and Router never raise to the agent; they return models with optional `error` field. Exceptions inside `mailbot-api` are fine internally but caught at verb/Router boundary and converted to `RouterError` / `<VerbName>Out(error=...)`.
- **AR-PAT-5** — **Prompt module structure:** each `prompts/<task_type>/vN.py` exports exactly four names — `VERSION` (str), `SYSTEM` (str, cacheable), `USER_TEMPLATE` (str), `OUTPUT_SCHEMA` (Pydantic class).
- **AR-PAT-6** — **Tooling enforced clean before commit:** `ruff check`, `ruff format`, `mypy --strict`. Ruff lint rules forbid `os.environ` outside `config.py`, forbid `print()` outside `scripts/`, forbid raw `requests.*` outside `sync/`, forbid `sqlite3.connect()` outside `db/`.

#### Deployment & Hermes Integration (Brainstorming Rules S–X)

- **AR-DEPLOY-1** — **3-container Docker Compose stack** on `mailbot-net` Docker network: `mailbot-hermes` (Hermes runtime, Discord gateway, cron, memory) + `mailbot-api` (Router, verbs, MCP server, sync worker, holds `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY`) + `ollama` (Qwen 3B + bge-small/nomic-embed-text model serving).
- **AR-DEPLOY-2** — **Hermes-native primitives used (no reinvention):** Discord gateway, `cron` (no_agent for sync, with_agent for digest/drift/sampling), `wakeAgent: false` for empty syncs, `context_from` chaining, `enabled_toolsets` per-cron narrowing, `fallback_providers` emergency safety net, persistent memory + Honcho, SOUL.md identity, AGENTS.md operational rules, MCP client to mailbot-api verb server, prompt caching, context compression — all pointed at Router.
- **AR-DEPLOY-3** — **`.env.example` lists all required keys with comments:** `DISCORD_BOT_TOKEN`, `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY`, `OUTLOOK_*` (CLIENT_ID/SECRET/TENANT/REFRESH_TOKEN), `MAILBOT_DB_PATH`, `OLLAMA_URL`, `MAILBOT_ROUTER_KEY`. `scripts/check_env.py` refuses to start service if any required key missing (Rule U).
- **AR-DEPLOY-4** — **Hermes `cron/jobs.json` seeds:** `daily_digest_0800` (agent-involving, calls `compose_digest`), `weekly_drift_sun_0900` (drift report), `weekly_sampling_sun_0930` (production sampling DMs).
- **AR-DEPLOY-5** — **VPS operator surface:** `scripts/mailbot` CLI installed on VPS (`status`, `logs`, `pause`, `resume`, `sync-now`, `replay <action_id>`, `revert <action_id>`); `scripts/setup_vps.sh` one-time bootstrap (Docker install, service user, volumes, perms); `scripts/deploy.sh` `docker save + scp + docker load + rolling restart`; `scripts/backup.sh` nightly SQLite `.backup` + config tarball + optional B2 rsync; `scripts/restore.sh` from backup tarball.

#### User-Model Storage (Architecture Gap Resolution)

- **AR-USERMODEL-1** — **Adam's user model lives in Hermes persistent memory**, not a parallel MailBot SQL `user_models` table. Output of `user_model_refresh` task written via Hermes memory APIs from agent's own task handling. `tone_style_mirror` and `importance_scoring` access it via Hermes context compression.

#### Analytics & Visualization (added 2026-05-31 per Adam's request)

- **AR-ANALYTICS-1** — **`mailbot-api` ships with `numpy` and `matplotlib`** for tabular analysis and chart rendering over the local mailbox + `router_calls`. These libraries are **not alternatives to the verb API or the Router**: any analysis that hits the LLM still goes through `ask_router`; any raw email body still goes through `hydrate_email` (Rule J); any chart that needs LLM-derived data reads cached derived-field columns (Rule A) — never re-derives. New "analytics verbs" live in `mailbot_api/verbs/analytics/` and return either structured Pydantic models OR a `(bytes, mime_type)` tuple for chart PNGs. Charts posted to Discord by Hermes are subject to the same chat-input redactor (FR-4.7) on any inline text labels rendered onto the image. `pandas` is explicitly deferred until a future story justifies it (most tabular shaping is cleaner as raw SQL).
- **AR-ANALYTICS-2** — **Chart rendering uses matplotlib's `Agg` non-interactive backend** (`matplotlib.use("Agg")`) — no GUI dependencies; rendering happens entirely off-screen in the worker process. Generated PNGs are returned as bytes (never written to disk on `mailbot-api`); Hermes posts them as Discord attachments. Standard chart dimensions: 1200×800 px @ 100 DPI (Discord-friendly, ~50–100 KB per PNG).

### UX Design Requirements

**N/A** — MailBot has no UX design document and no visual UI surface. Discord (via Hermes adapter) is the only human interface (Rule X). Slash commands (`/cost`, `/pause`, `/resume`, `/cancel`, `/mute`, `/label`, `/budget reset`, `/confirm`) and conversational chat are the entire interaction surface. Notification tiering (FR-7.4 urgent/important/informational/silent), digest format (FR-7.7), persona consistency (FR-4.6, NFR-PERSONA-1..3 — defender voice anchored in `SOUL.md`), and anti-fatigue mechanics (FR-7.5) carry the responsibility that UX patterns would normally cover. There are no design tokens, components, breakpoints, animations, or accessibility patterns to implement.
